# SynthyBoy MPC Build Guide

## Build

The MPC package holds two builds of the same VST2 module: a 32-bit ARMv7 hard-float build with NEON (`VST/synthyboy-arm32.so`, `file32`: the MPC Key 61 and the other 32-bit MPCs) and a 64-bit AArch64 build (`VST/synthyboy-arm64.so`, `file64`: the MPC XL and the other 64-bit MPCs). The MPC loads the one that matches its CPU. The build script is `scripts/build-mpc.sh`; the host needs Docker. The environment variable `MPC_SDK` must point at a checkout of the MPC plugin SDK (it is not part of this repository and must not be copied into it). The environment variable `MPC_BUILD_IMAGE` (or the older `ARMV7_IMAGE`) selects the Docker image (default: the pinned Debian 12 image in the script); an image that already has the cross toolchains installed is faster because the script skips the `apt-get install` step inside the container.

The exact command line is `scripts/build-mpc.sh [--arch arm32|arm64|all] [--out DIR] [--jobs N] [--build-dir DIR] [--skins DIR] [--bench] [--release] [--skin-only] [--no-validate]`; `--arch` defaults to `all`. The plugin is always built with the overlay (and on ARM the GPU helper `VST/synthyboy-overlay-gpu-<arch>`); there is no switch or CMake option for it. `--skin-only` copies `skin/` and runs the manifest and validation steps into `<out>/` without Docker or C++ cross build. The script does the following, step by step:

**One step device build (owner, 2026-11-28).** `scripts/build-k61.sh` (Key 61, armv7, Key 61 preset set) and `scripts/build-xl.sh` (XL, aarch64, XL preset set) build ONE architecture with `build-mpc.sh`, move the package into `.hw-local/mpc-builds/<branch>-<commit>[-dirty-<time>]-<arch>[-release]/` next to the main checkout, add its line (branch, commit, architecture, date, md5 of the binary) to that folder's `README.md`, then install it with `k61-install.sh` / `xl-install.sh`. Options: `--release` (build-mpc.sh `--release`), `--no-install`, `--name NAME`, `--jobs N` (default: the CPUs of the machine), `-- ARGS` passed to build-mpc.sh. `MPC_SDK` defaults to `.hw-local/mpc-knowledge/docs-official/mpc-sdk`, `MPC_BUILDS` to `.hw-local/mpc-builds`. An archived folder is never overwritten, so the installer's missing backup is covered by the archive. Shared code: `scripts/build-and-install.sh`.

1. For each architecture it runs a `docker run` container (running `scripts/mpc-container-build.sh`) mounting the source tree read-only, the build tree of that architecture (`<build-dir>/arm32`, `<build-dir>/arm64`), the output directory, and the SDK.
2. Inside the container it installs missing packages (the cross compiler, the matching `binutils`, `cmake`, `file`, `git`, `ninja` or `make`).
3. It runs `cmake` with the toolchain file `cmake/toolchains/armv7-linux-gnueabihf.cmake` (`-march=armv7-a -mfpu=neon -mfloat-abi=hard`) or `cmake/toolchains/aarch64-linux-gnu.cmake` (`-march=armv8-a`), with ``, `-DSYNTHYBOY_BUILD_TESTS=OFF`, `-DSYNTHYBOY_BUILD_MPC_SHELL=ON`. The package is the own engine.
4. It builds the `synthyboy_mpc` target.
5. It checks, with the same checks for both architectures, that the `.so` is an ELF file of the right class and machine (`file`; for arm32 also `readelf -A`: hard-float), that it needs only `libc`, `libm`, `libdl`, `libpthread`, and the dynamic loader (`ld-linux-armhf` / `ld-linux-aarch64`), that it references no GLIBC symbol version above 2.39 (the glibc of the MPC OS 3 firmware; Debian 12 has 2.36) and no dynamic `libstdc++`, and that `VSTPluginMain` is the only exported symbol (using `nm -D`).
6. It produces a stripped copy (`synthyboy_mpc.stripped.so`) for the package, checks it again (same checks, and the exports after strip); the unstripped module stays in the build tree for profiling.
7. On the host it assembles ONE plugin folder `Safian - VST - SynthyBoy` with `VST/synthyboy-arm32.so` and `VST/synthyboy-arm64.so` (the stripped copies of this run only; `VST/` is emptied first) and `Content/presets` (a copy of `content/presets`).
8. It copies the committed skin `skin/` into `Plugin Skins` (edited directly, never generated), unless `--skins DIR` is given.
9. It copies the committed `mpc-package/plugin.json` and `version.xml` (only the binaries that were built stay in `plugin.json`).
10. It runs the SDK's `tools/validate.py` on the finished folder. With `--arch all` the validator must report no error. The validator's schema lists `file32` and `file64` as required, while the SDK's `plugin-folder-format.md` says a 32-bit MPC uses only `file32` and a 64-bit one only `file64`; a single-architecture build (`--arch arm32` or `--arch arm64`) accepts exactly the one finding about the key of the architecture that was not built and fails on any other error.

### MPC XL (64-bit, AArch64)

The MPC XL has four Cortex-A55 and four Cortex-A76 cores and runs only the 64-bit loader (`/lib/ld-linux-aarch64.so.1`, libstdc++ 6.0.32). The arm64 module is built with the Debian 12 `g++-aarch64-linux-gnu` (glibc 2.36, below the 2.39 limit of the SDK validator), `-march=armv8-a` (no newer architecture level, no `-mcpu=native`, so it runs on the A55 and the A76 cores), `-static-libstdc++ -static-libgcc` and hidden visibility, the same link options as the arm32 module. `scripts/build-mpc.sh --arch arm64` builds only this one (the folder then has `file64` only).

The NEON loops of the own engine (`core/engine-own/dsp/simd.h`, `SB_NEON`) are compiled on arm64 Linux as well as on arm32 (not on Apple's arm64: macOS and iOS keep the scalar code). Like on arm32 the translation units that use them are compiled with `fp-contract=off`, so the NEON code and the scalar code (`-DSB_NO_NEON`) give the same samples to the last bit: all 112 factory presets rendered at tier low with `synthyboy_preset_render` gave identical WAV files with NEON and with `SB_NO_NEON` on AArch64, and so the arm64 module sounds like the arm32 one. (The arm64 scalar code that was used before, with the compiler's fused multiply-add, differs from both in the last bits.)

`--bench` also builds `synthyboy_bench` and `synthyboy_noteon_bench` with the same toolchain (a separate CMake tree `<build-dir>/<arch>-bench` with `-DSYNTHYBOY_BUILD_TESTS=ON`, libstdc++ linked statically) and puts them into `<out>/bench-arm32/` and `<out>/bench-arm64/`, outside the plugin folder; see Benchmark switches for what they do on the device.

The module must be built with GCC, not with Zig: `--exclude-libs,ALL` is not understood by Zig's linker and a Zig-built module crashed at host exit on the device.

The native acceptance is `sh shell-mpc/accept.sh` with `MPC_SDK` set. It builds the module natively on the development machine, runs the SDK host simulator with tests, checks the parameter table against `docs/params.json`, verifies the exported symbols, tests the factory presets as programs, runs the stub host for state round-trips, and builds and runs with ThreadSanitizer to check for data races.

## Package layout

The output folder is named `Safian - VST - SynthyBoy` and contains:

- `plugin.json` — the MPC manifest with `file32` (`VST/synthyboy-arm32.so`) and `file64` (`VST/synthyboy-arm64.so`); a key is written only for a binary that exists.
- `version.xml` — the content version of the package (the root manifest version with a fourth part) and, when `Plugin Skins` is present, the `uiversion` of the skins.
- `VST/synthyboy-arm32.so` — the stripped 32-bit ARMv7 shared object that exports only `VSTPluginMain`.
- `VST/synthyboy-arm64.so` — the stripped 64-bit AArch64 shared object that exports only `VSTPluginMain`.
- `VST/synthyboy-overlay-gpu-arm32` / `VST/synthyboy-overlay-gpu-arm64` — in every ARM package (the overlay is always built in, see "Overlay (experimental)"): the executable of the GPU helper, stripped, next to the plugin binary of its architecture. It is not in `plugin.json` (the host never loads it; the plugin starts it). The production package has no such file.
- `Content/presets` — the factory presets from `content/presets`, shown as programs in the MPC's preset browser.
- `Presets` — Akai Plugin Program (`.xpl`) preset files for the host's Sounds browser, committed in `content/mpc-presets/` (copied by `build-mpc.sh`, edited directly). The naming rule is `NNN-<Category>-<Name>.xpl` (3-digit zero-padded category order index, category name, and FAT/exFAT-sanitized preset name, e.g. `000-Bass-Space Wobble Bass.xpl`). The Akai host's Sounds browser indexes presets directly from this folder when present in the package. Each file specifies the target plugin path via the `<PLUGIN file="...">` attribute: on the MPC XL `/nvme/VST2 Plugins/Safian - VST - SynthyBoy/VST/synthyboy-arm64.so` and on the MPC Key 61 `/sdcard/VST2 Plugins/Safian - VST - SynthyBoy/VST/synthyboy-arm32.so`. In `scripts/build-mpc.sh` under `XPL_PATH_MODE="device"` (default), the XL presets are packaged inside `Presets/` and a Key 61 set is written to `Presets-k61/` next to the package folder; `scripts/mpc-fix-xpl-paths.py` allows rewriting these paths for installer targets. If the host resolves presets without requiring an exact path match, switching to a single shared set is a one-line change (`XPL_PATH_MODE=shared`).
- `Content/packs/<pack id>/` — the factory content packs `content/factory-wavetables` (16 wavetables) and `content/factory-samples` (12 samples, 3 multisample instruments with their SFZ files, 3 impulse responses for Convolve), written by `scripts/gen-content-pack.py` (CC0-1.0, synthesised, 11.5 MB in all) and referenced by pack id + file + SHA-256 from the presets of sound set batch 2 (`content.wavetables / samples / impulses / instruments`). The plugin's content store reads them from there (`addPackRoot (Content/packs)` in `shell-mpc/synthyboy_mpc.cpp`); a missing file is skipped on load (a wavetable becomes the saw, a sample, granular, spectral or multisample oscillator is silent, Convolve is bypassed).
- `Plugin Skins` — the touch-screen UI and Q-Links knob mapping, committed in `skin/` (edited directly, no generation), including the browser artwork tile (`Plugin Skins/browser_images/soundsmode.png`) for Sounds > INSTRUMENTS.

Skin install checks (`scripts/validate_synthyboy_skin.py`, run by `scripts/install-mpc-package.sh` before every install):
- Every `Parameter N` handle in `TUI.json` must be below the plugin parameter count: `docs/params.json` count plus the MPC extras that `shell-mpc/synthyboy_mpc.cpp` adds to `numParams`, computed by compiling `scripts/skin-param-extra.cpp` against the committed headers (needs `c++`; no binary is read).
- Every `localComponentDefinitions` entry (the union of `TUI.json` and its `defs_*.json` import files) is compared by key (sha256 of its canonical JSON) with the known-good `scripts/skin-baseline/TUI.keys.json`: added keys pass, changed or removed keys fail with the list.
- An intended edit needs `SYNTHYBOY_SKIN_ACCEPT_CHANGES=1` for that run.
- After the owner has proven the skin on the Key 61, run `scripts/skin-baseline-update.py` and commit the new baseline.

Skin format schema (`schemas/skin-tui.schema.json`, `schemas/skin-qlinks.schema.json`, JSON Schema draft-07, checked first by the same script with `jsonschema`; a missing `jsonschema` fails the run):
- The host drops the WHOLE `TUI.json` (blank page) for a malformed shape and says nothing, so every shape is checked, not only the ones that once failed. The schema follows the shapes of the skin that runs on both devices (git `1fd0d592:skin/TUI.json`) and the SDK `docs/ui-reference.md`; `additionalProperties` is false for every object whose field set the working skin proves.
- Every finding prints with its JSON path (`ERROR $.pageData.tabs[0]...`); errors exit non-zero. Duplicate keys are errors, except the proven duplicate `version` key in the data of an overlay widget (`MenuOverlay`, `NumericOverlay`; the first one wins when validating).
- Rules found so far: `additionalInvalidatingHandles` is a flat array of strings (a nested list blanks the Key 61); an `Image` is `version 2` with `imageType`, `colour`, `image` (`version 1` has only `colour` and `image`; `version 1` with `imageType` and no `colour` is rejected); handles follow the grammar in the schema (a `/`-prefix that is not a known modifier fails); `Button` is `version 2` with all keys; `bounds` is four numbers; colours are `AARRGGBB` or `0`.
- Semantic checks in code (`tui_semantics`): a component `type` is a built-in or a local definition key; definition keys are unique and do not shadow built-ins; image files exist; `Show Overlay` names a local definition; `IndexedEnabling/i/n` has `i < n`, `RangedEnabling/a/b/n` has `a <= b < n`, and `n` equals the range of a `Local/n/...` base; function key plus sub-tab positions are unique.
- Warnings (accepted but not proven on a device): SDK-documented-only handle modifiers (`ResetOnGestureEnd/`, `Hold/`, `Invert/`, ...), a `Local` id other than `synthyboyWarpPage`, `Button` `buttonId` above 98 or `numButtonsInGroup` above 99, SDK-documented-only component types, more than 37 tabs (the SDK note says 32; the proven skin runs 37).
- A new host finding (a form the host accepts or drops) is added as a schema rule, or moves from the warning list to the proven forms once the owner has proven it on a device. Check without the other install checks: `scripts/validate_synthyboy_skin.py --schema-only <package or folder with TUI.json and Q-Links.json>`.

The plugin finds its own folder by walking up from the binary's path (obtained via `dladdr` on `VSTPluginMain`) to the first directory that contains a file named `plugin.json`. This logic is in `shell-mpc/plugin_paths.h`.

## Install on the MPC

Copy the whole folder `Safian - VST - SynthyBoy` into a folder named exactly `VST2 Plugins` at the root of a USB drive, SD card, or the MPC's internal drive. The MPC must be running MPC OS 3.10.1 or later. In **Preferences → Enhancements (Beta)** turn on both **Enable Enhancements** and **External Plugin Support**; the plugin stays hidden until both switches are on. After inserting the drive, open the plugin browser (instrument track → plugin program → browse, or an insert slot). The plugin is listed under **External Plugins**.

Replacing a plugin: copy the new folder over the old one, then remove and re-add the plugin on the track (or restart the MPC if the skins changed). Uninstalling: delete the folder from `VST2 Plugins`. Projects that used it will load without the plugin.

## Known host behaviour

### Observed on the device

Seen on an MPC Key 61 with firmware 3.10.1:

- The host runs plugins at 44.1 kHz with 128 frame blocks.
- There is no plugin GUI. The screen pages come from Plugin Skins (`TUI.json`), and the hardware knobs are mapped from `Q-Links.json`.
- A newly copied plugin appears only after the MPC application is restarted.
- Newly copied or modified presets in the package `Presets/` folder appear in the Sounds browser only after the MPC application is restarted or the device is rebooted.
- The plugin runs inside the MPC's process. For that reason the shell wraps the instance creation and every dispatch call in `catch (...)` (no instance, or a result of 0), so no exception leaves the plugin.
- A module built with Zig crashed when the host exited (see Build): the module is built with GCC.
- The packaged plugin loads in the stock MPC app; stepping through the presets works; the generated skin with its 30 pages works (the skin of that check had 30 pages, one function key each; the later skins with the Env, Osc and LFO tab layouts and the sub-tabs were tried one by one, see "Function keys and sub-tabs"); the owner checked the sound by ear (it plays without crackling).

Measured on 2026-11-13 with the bench on the device (MPC Key 61, 44.1 kHz, 128 frame blocks, so one block lasts 2.902 ms; tier low, own engine):

- Basic patch, 8 notes held: block time average 0.535 ms, 99th percentile 0.789 ms, worst 1.405 ms.
- Idle (no notes): 0.022 ms per block. Each additional note costs about 0.070 ms per block.
- Note-on of 8 notes: average 0.97 ms, worst 1.34 ms.
- Preparing the engine (prepare) takes 542 ms.
- Heavy patch (3 oscillators, 2 filters, effects, 8 notes; the patch asks for 7 unison voices per oscillator, tier low ran 4): average 5.41 ms, 99th percentile 8.09 ms, worst 9.20 ms, which is longer than one block.
- The bench option `--ftz` makes no difference to these times.

Measured on 2026-11-13 in the afternoon with a unison cap in the engine (engine commit `c3a2065`), the same heavy patch with 8 notes, block time average / 99th percentile / worst:

- Cap 1: 3.73 / 5.46 / 6.04 ms.
- Cap 2: 4.16 / 5.43 / 5.67 ms.
- Cap 3: 5.36 / 8.27 / 8.98 ms.
- Cap 4: 5.97 / 9.00 / 9.62 ms.
- After one optimisation round (engine commit `4cd77ce`): cap 3: 4.64 / 5.52 / 5.66 ms; cap 1: 3.64 / 4.85 / 5.40 ms; the basic patch with 8 notes: 0.559 / 0.781 / 1.016 ms.

Measured on 2026-11-13 in the evening, after the later optimisation rounds (the final state: engine commit `91f2d75`, shell branch commit `17d6e11`), with the bench option `--preset-dir` on all 112 factory presets, 8 notes, tier low, 2 s per preset, notes retriggered after 1 s. Average of the 112 block-time averages, and how many presets have a 99th percentile above 2.0 ms or a worst block above 2.902 ms, per optimisation round (engine commit):

| Engine commit | Average of averages | 99th percentile above 2.9 ms | 99th percentile above 2.0 ms | Worst block above 2.902 ms |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `bc0f3ce` | 1.20 ms | 9 | 67 | 55 |
| `e3abe08` | 1.06 ms | 6 | 44 | 57 |
| `178316e` | 1.00 ms | 4 | 29 | 29 |
| `e596f94` | 0.98 ms | 0 | 6 | 35 |
| `91f2d75` | 0.87 ms | 0 | 0 | 0 |

In the final state, 8 notes: no preset has a 99th percentile above 2.0 ms and none has a worst block above 2.902 ms; six presets have a worst block above 2.0 ms, listed as average / 99th percentile / worst: Diffusor Cloud Pad 1.75 / 1.96 / 2.74 ms, Scream Filter Bass 1.63 / 1.86 / 2.36 ms, EMS Squelch Bass 1.50 / 1.71 / 2.24 ms, Diode Saturated Bass 1.44 / 1.66 / 2.13 ms, Twin Warp Lead 1.23 / 1.52 / 2.05 ms, Formant Vowel Bass 1.34 / 1.60 / 2.01 ms. With 4 notes the average of the averages is 0.666 ms and two presets have a worst block above 2.0 ms. The per-preset results of every round are kept as CSV files next to the measurement notes of the project.

Final state, other bench results (2026-11-13): the note-on of 8 notes takes 0.715 ms on average and 1.018 ms in the worst case (before the last round: 0.97 / 1.34 ms), 1 note 0.122 / 0.371 ms. The chorus section of a block went from 0.359 to 0.201 ms. In the slowest 1 % of the blocks of Moog Ladder Bass the number of voice render calls per block is 33.3 (all blocks: 32.0), so a note-on block no longer runs more voices than a held block.

What follows from these numbers:

- The synthetic heavy patch with 8 notes does not fit into the 2.902 ms block even without unison (cap 1). The earlier measurement of it, at engine commit `878326f`, was cap 3: average 4.42 ms, 99th percentile 6.35 ms, worst 6.83 ms. In the final state (engine commit `91f2d75`, tier low, unison cap 3, 3 s, retrigger after 1000 s, so no retrigger inside the run) the heavy patch gives, as average / 99th percentile / worst block: 8 notes 3.354 / 3.691 / 3.959 ms, 6 notes 2.637 / 2.868 / 3.781 ms, 4 notes 2.059 / 2.595 / 3.205 ms. The basic patch with 8 notes (10 s) gives 0.474 / 0.703 / 0.965 ms. So the heavy patch does not fit safely with 8 or 6 notes (with 8 notes even the average is above the block; with 6 notes the average and the 99th percentile are just below it and the worst block is above it); with 4 notes the average and the 99th percentile are below the block and the worst block is above it. The factory presets are lighter than the synthetic heavy patch.
- The 112 factory presets do fit with 8 notes (see the table above), so no cap on the number of notes is needed. No decision about such a cap is pending.
- The default of the unison cap is 3; that there is a cap is the owner's decision. A cap of 3 affects 14 of the 112 factory presets: Fat Reese, Cosmic Drift Lead, Hyper Saw Lead, Diffusor Cloud Pad, Ethereal Glass, Frozen Aurora, Harmonic Cloud, Infinite Horizon, Lush Analog Pad, Space Odyssey Pad, Tri-Band Split Texture, Velvet Strings, Vintage Brass Pad, Pizzicato Strings. The heaviest factory preset has 2 oscillators with 7 unison voices each (Hyper Saw Lead).

Saving a project and loading it again, tried on 2026-11-13 in the afternoon:

- After the MPC application was restarted, the project (Sbtest) loaded with the preset name "Acid Saw Reso *" and the edited values: Osc 1 Level 79.4, Filter 1 Cutoff 450, Resonance 78. Nobody listened to the sound after loading.
- With the plugin loaded and idle, the MPC process uses 4 to 7 % of the CPU.

LFO pictures, tried on the Key 61 on 2026-11-13 with the first version of the pictures (208 x 38 pixel film strips, one per shape, on the tabs LFO 1-2 and LFO 3-4; the observations about the host hold for both) and the first build of the display, which refreshed 25 times a second and ran its thread at nice +10 (the default is 10 a second and a normal thread since then, see "LFO pictures" below; the observations in this list were not repeated with it, the audio callback measurement below was):

- The host redraws a control bound to a plugin parameter only when the plugin calls `audioMasterAutomate` for it. A parameter that the plugin changes without that call stayed at its old value on the screen (value text and knob) for the whole test: the host does not poll `getParameter`.
- A `Plotter` bound to such a parameter (`Parameter N`, with and without history) stayed an empty grey box, also when the plugin called `audioMasterAutomate`. A film-strip `Image` bound to the parameter moves.
- The picture of the selected LFO shape (a film strip per shape, shown by `IndexedEnabling` on the shape parameter) switches with the shape; with the cursor stepping, the cursor line and dot move from picture to picture.
- A freshly loaded preset shows no `*` next to its name after 8 seconds of moving cursors: the display parameters do not mark the preset as modified.
- CPU of the MPC process (20 second mean, percent of one core): normal build, LFO tab open, no note 26 %; self-test build with four moving cursors, LFO tab open 29 %; self-test build, another tab open 26 %. So the display costs about 3 percentage points, and only while the tab is visible.
- The host unpacks every film strip into `/var/tmp/filmstrips` (decoded size counts): with the skin loaded 174 files, 422 MB; without the LFO pictures 173 files, 417 MB. The four LFOs share the same files and the host unpacks them once.
- Recording into automation could not be tried by touch (the owner tries it); the display parameters report `effCanBeAutomated` 0.

Audio callback gaps with the display running, measured on 2026-11-13 with a statistics build (the shell records the time between two calls of `pluginProcessReplacing` and the time spent in it), on the new thread model of the display ("LFO live display and the display thread"). Conditions: tab LFO 1 open, no note sounding (the measurement does not cover sounding voices), 44.1 kHz, 128 frames, nominal block time 2.902 ms, one run of 50 seconds on the Key 61 and 65 seconds on the MPC XL (the raw statistics of every run were kept). Variants: the display off (`SYNTHYBOY_MPC_LFO_LIVE` OFF), and the self-test (four cursors that move all the time) at 10, 25 and 50 steps a second.

- Key 61 (arm32): 15 909 to 16 000 callbacks a run; in all four variants every gap between two calls is in the bucket below 1.5 times the block time (none at 1.5 times or more). The largest gap is 3.525 ms with the display off (1.21 times) and 3.292, 3.300 and 3.237 ms with the self-test at 10, 25 and 50 a second (1.13, 1.14, 1.12 times), so between 3.2 and 3.5 ms (1.12 to 1.21 times) whatever the variant. Time spent in the callback: average 63 to 65 microseconds, longest 1.27 to 1.31 ms.
- MPC XL (arm64): 22 142 to 22 154 callbacks a run; again every gap in the bucket below 1.5 times the block time. The largest gap is 3.234 ms with the display off (1.11 times) and 3.295, 3.282 and 3.273 ms at 10, 25 and 50 a second (1.14, 1.13, 1.13 times), about 3.2 to 3.3 ms (1.11 to 1.14 times). Time spent in the callback: average 29 to 30 microseconds, longest 0.41 to 0.44 ms.
- So the report of the display at 10, 25 and 50 steps a second, with four cursors moving all the time, did not hold up the audio callback on either machine; the gaps are the same as with the display off. This was measured without sound (the four runs per machine above).
- With sounding voices (MPC XL, the night of 2026-11-13, measuring build `live-10`, tab LFO 1 open): a four voice chord held for 14 seconds, sent to the MIDI input of the app. The cursor of the LFO picture stood at six different places in six consecutive screenshots, so it followed the sounding voice. The statistics file of that run counts about 434 000 callbacks, but that is the total since the plugin was loaded (about 21 minutes of running at 2.902 ms a block), not the callbacks of the chord, which were about 4800. Over the whole run, into which the 14 second chord fell, every callback is in the bucket below 1.5 times the block time, the largest gap is 3290 microseconds and the longest time spent in the callback is 414 microseconds. The file cannot separate the chord from the rest of the run, so these numbers hold for the run as a whole and not for the chord alone. With sounding voices on the Key 61 see the spectrum measurements below.
- The transparent cursor strip over the shape picture is drawn on both machines (a white vertical line on the picture), and the Rate / Division change with Sync works on both machines.

Native widgets bound to a plugin parameter, tried on the Key 61 on the night of 2026-11-13 (MPC 3.10.1.12, measuring build `selftest-10`, the skin of that time with one added trial element in the empty bottom row of the Macros tab, bound to `Parameter 2637`, "LFO 1 Cursor"; the trial skins did not stay on the machine):

- A `Meter` with exactly the key set and order of the `envfollower_meter1` element of the stock Multiband Filterbank skin (`version 3`, `direction "Right"`, `invert false`, `inactiveImage`, `rmsImage ""`, `rmsHandle "Invalid"`, `peakImage`, `peakHandle "Parameter 2637"`, `peakHoldHandle "Hold/Parameter 2637"`, `peakHoldSize 0`, `peakHoldColour`), two own pictures of 400 x 24 pixels, the element's remap `Data -> Parameter 2637`, bounds `40 500 400 24`: the page stayed whole and the bar stood at about 81 %, 16 % and 43 % of its length in three pictures about 3 seconds apart, so a Meter bound to a plugin parameter follows the value and is redrawn. The vertical direction (`direction "Up"`, used by the output meters of the stock skin), 32 meters on one tab and the Meter on the MPC XL were not tried then; they were tried later the same night, see the next list.
- A `Plotter` with the exact key set of the `spectrum_plot` element of that skin stayed empty with `dataHandle "Parameter 2637"` (with and without history) and with `dataHandle "Data"` remapped to `Parameter 2637`. The stock plugins feed it an array over `Info/Named/...`, an external plugin has no known way to that channel.

Spectrum display (section "Spectrum"), measured on the night of 2026-11-13 to 2026-11-14 with a statistics build (MPC 3.10.1.12, 44.1 kHz, 128 frames, nominal block time 2.902 ms; the raw statistics files of every run were kept). The statistics are the audio callback's, not the display thread's: the FFT runs on the display thread.

- Key 61 (arm32), one `Meter` with `direction "Up"` bound to a plugin parameter (build with the LFO self-test cursor, no spectrum code, 2641 parameters): the vertical Meter works. The statistics of that run: 12 420 callbacks, every gap in the bucket below 1.5 times the block time, largest gap 3503 microseconds (1.21 times), time in the callback 67.9 microseconds on average, 1285 microseconds at most.
- Key 61, the 32 Meters of the spectrum driven by the self-test wave (self-test wave build, no sound), at 10 steps a second: 12 418 callbacks, every gap below 1.5 times the block time, largest gap 3650 microseconds (1.26 times), callback average 68.4 and longest 1339 microseconds. At 25 steps a second (32 reports every 40 milliseconds): 12 521 callbacks, every gap below 1.5 times, largest gap 3953 microseconds (1.36 times), callback average 69.8 and longest 1312 microseconds. So 32 meters moving all the time did not hold up the audio callback at either rate; the largest gap grows a little with the rate.
- Key 61, a real six note chord with the normal spectrum (analyser on the output, 10 a second, no self-test): the statistics file was written 20 seconds after the plugin was created and counts 5761 callbacks (the file does not say how long the chord was held), every gap below 1.5 times the block time, largest gap 3421 microseconds (1.18 times), time in the callback 514 microseconds on average (the sounding voices: the self-test runs above are about 68), longest 2080 microseconds, which is below the 2.902 ms block.
- MPC XL (arm64), the 32 Meters with the self-test wave at 25 steps a second, the surface with the meters visible: 13 623 callbacks, every gap below 1.5 times the block time, largest gap 3283 microseconds (1.13 times), callback average 13.7 and longest 110 microseconds.
- Not measured yet: how the peak hold of the Meters looks on the screen (the element has `peakHoldSize 0`), the MPC XL with real sound in the test project, and the CPU cost of the FFT on its own (the statistics above do not contain it, it runs on the display thread).
Overlay (section "Overlay (experimental)"), measured on the night of 2026-11-14 on an MPC Key 61 (firmware 3.10.1.12, 44.1 kHz, project `Sbtest`, preset `Acid Saw Reso`) with the arm32 build and the skin of `skin/TUI.json`; the status files and the kernel state of the plane were read over ssh, the screenshots (primary plane only) were made with `mpc-tools/shot.sh`:

- The host loads the skin with 35 tabs in 10 function keys (the `Filter` key has three sub-tabs: Filter 1, F1 Model, Filter 2). The tab area of a plugin page starts at screen y 110 (not 108): the marker squares drawn at tab y 24 are at screen y 134 in the screenshot, every pixel exactly the colour of the PNG.
- The page detector reads the markers exactly (`markers_read` equals the expected 103a2c and 3a102f) with the mapping rotation 90 (UI pixel (X, Y) at panel (Y, 1279 - X)); that proves the orientation of the primary plane. The plane 38 state while the Filter 1 tab is on screen: `crtc=crtc-0`, `fb` allocated by `sb-overlay`, format `AR24`, `crtc-pos=232x1168+124+56`, `src-pos=232x1168+0+0`, `normalized-zpos=2`; plane 40 `crtc=(null)`, planes 33 and 35 untouched.
- Tab changes: from the end of the tap to the plane being on or off in the kernel state 150 to 170 milliseconds (the host's own redraw is in that time); the full screen menu of a selector (double tap on a value) takes the plane off, closing it brings it back. The files are read once a second: the kill switch file took the plane off after 0.07 seconds and back after 1.02 seconds, the rotation file `270` took it off after 0.99 seconds (the markers are not where that mapping looks) and `90` back after 1.01 seconds.
- The curve is the real one: for the dumped 256 values at Ladder, Smear + Style E (band peak notch) and after dragging the Cutoff knob the largest difference to `synthyboy_filter_response --set ...` for the same preset, model, style, cutoff and resonance is 0.003 dB (the printed precision); changing Model or Style changes the curve hash in the status file, dragging the Cutoff knob (450.0 to 12628.3 and back to 444.9 in the screenshots) changes the hash and the value in the screenshot.
- Cost of one full computation of the curve (`shell-mpc/overlay/tests/compute_bench.cpp`, static arm32 build, 44.1 kHz, 10 repetitions per model): the median over the 27 models of filter 1 is 11.9 milliseconds, the fastest model 4.2, the slowest (Smear) 23.1; Analog 4.5, Multi SVF 14.9.
- With the LFO 1 (2 Hz) routed to Filter 1 Cutoff (Mod 01 amount 36) and a held note: 30 pictures a second, 8 full computations a second, the rest translated; per picture 3.8 milliseconds drawing, 2.4 rotating, the SETPLANE call waits 11.5 (the vblank). CPU of the overlay thread: 21.1 percent of one core while the curve moves, 0.2 percent on the Filter 1 tab at rest and on another tab (it only polls). The thread ran on cores 0 and 1 in 50 samples a run, its allowed CPUs are 0-1 (`/sys/devices/system/cpu/isolated` is 2-3). The whole MPC process: 28 percent of one core at rest on that tab, 65 percent with a held note and the moving curve, 40 percent with a held note on another tab (the synthesis itself).
- 15 minutes (900.8 seconds) with a note of 12 seconds every 30 seconds on the Filter 1 tab: 30 notes, the MPC process id unchanged, `VmRSS` of the process 948244 kB at the start and at every one of the 30 samples and at the end, free space on `/data` 2.4 GB before and after, the overlay thread 8.8 percent of one core averaged over the run (21 percent during the notes), no new kernel message (`dmesg`). After a note the live cutoff is the knob value again and the curve hash equals the one before the note.
- Not measured: two plugin instances, a long run of hours, the MPC XL, anything the eye has to judge (orientation of the picture, smoothness, blending, exact position on the glass).

- Overlay with several views, measured on the Key 61 on 2026-11-14 (night, arm32 md5 `e3828c02e2ba73d708f275663f8168d2`, 44.1 kHz, project `Sbtest`, preset `Acid Saw Reso`; the 36 tab skin loads, the `Filter` key shows four sub-tabs). Each of the nine picture views (Filter 1 / 2, LFO 1 - 4, Osc 1 - 3) and the four envelope views was visible on its tab with the kernel state of plane 38 equal to the region of the layout (`crtc-pos` 232x1168+124+56 for the filters, 240x600+140+340 for the LFOs, 240x512+140+384 for the oscillators, 222x1230+178+27 for the envelopes), gone on Macros, Sub/Nois, the sub-tabs `F1 Model` and `Osc1 Shp` and under a full screen menu. Data: the 256 values of Filter 2 differ from `synthyboy_filter_response --filter 2 --sr 44100` by at most 0.0005 dB, the 300 values of an LFO shape from `LfoDisplay` of the same settings by at most 0.0007, the 512 values of an oscillator cycle from `OscCycle` by 0 (also for a frame position modulated by an LFO during a held note: 65.6, -16.4, 19.6, 37.5 percent against the knob 33.3 percent). The LFO cursor advanced 0.183 cycles per second at the Rate 0.18. The envelope dot passed the stages attack, decay, sustain, release in order (x 60, 278, 345, 457, 579 on the screen); of 215 checks of the line colour 56 were hits at the point itself and 159 were accepted by the verification of the visible stretches of the polyline (the handles hide the line at the corners), none was a miss. Thread CPU (one core, status file): at rest 0.2 percent in every view; while moving about 22 percent for the Filter 1 curve (LFO to cutoff), 4.6 to 5.8 percent for an LFO cursor, 11.3 percent for a modulated oscillator, 8.7 to 8.9 percent for an envelope dot. The soak of 903 seconds cycling all 13 views with a note of 3 seconds in each (165 samples): the MPC pid stayed 31525, `VmRSS` between 946476 and 948444 kB, no new `dmesg` line, `/data` free 2.4 GB. Not seen by any test: how the pictures look (orientation, smoothness, blending, the dot on the line).
- Overlay drawn by the GPU in the helper process (section "Overlay (experimental)", "GPU drawing in a helper process"), measured on the Key 61 on 2026-11-14 (morning, arm32; measuring builds: plugin md5 `6d748e4e3684f79d21a70fc6dfbc10c3` with helper `a120acec716abcae4ae05727e5ab796a` for the load numbers and the fault tests, the same code with the callback statistics for the audio test, final build plugin `5fc9dab8f0800225f5890cd1c4c1393a` with helper `a050de851c267357474eaae6d9bacee6` for the soak; 44.1 kHz, project `Sbtest`, preset `Acid Saw Reso`, one instance). Before anything was built: a plain test program that borrowed the application's `/dev/dri/card0` descriptor, started a child with `posix_spawn` and sent the descriptor over a socket with `SCM_RIGHTS` found it with master rights in the child (`DRM_IOCTL_AUTH_MAGIC` answers EINVAL) and the child set and removed plane 38 with a dumb buffer (return 0 both times): the same open file description carries the application's rights.
- Overlay with several instances (section "Overlay (experimental)", "Several instances"), measured on the Key 61 on 2026-11-14 (arm32 plugin md5 `8ce147c84555b58235c8460849bffb9d`, helper `a050de851c267357474eaae6d9bacee6`, project `Sbtest`, ONE instance; a second track could not be added with the device's preferences, so the proof is the host's redraw with other values through the debug file, not two real instances). The host draws the film strip frame of the reported value exactly: in a screenshot of the primary plane every pixel of the instance picture has the colour of the table on all 13 view tabs, at the position and size of the layout (the ring of pixels around it does not), with `instance_slot: 0`, `instance_pixel_read: 1414d8`, `renderer: gpu`, and the plane at the region of the view; Macros and Sub tabs: plane off. With the debug file `/tmp/synthyboy-overlay-fake-slot` the host redraws the picture (read 3 seconds after each change) with the colour of slot 1 (`3e14d8`), 3 (`9214d8`), 7 (`923ed8`), 15 (`9292d8`), none (`c814d8`) and blank (`14c8d8`), each read back exactly; with a slot nobody holds the overlay is hidden (`hidden: instance slot N is not alive`), with the none frame `hidden: instance has no slot (more than 16 instances)`, with the blank frame `hidden: instance of the page not identified` and the report requests follow the doubling gap (4 requests in the first 3 seconds, then one about every 3.2 seconds, 11 in all after 27 seconds). With the `stale` file the overlay went off 0.36 s after the file appeared and was back 0.12 s later (the plugin was asked, reported, the host redrew). The first visit of a view tab after an app start (film strips decoded then): the plane was on 0.27 s after the touch, with no request needed (`instance_rereports: 0`); the project does not open on a view tab, so the overlay needs one touch to a view tab after the load. At rest the overlay thread used 0.20 % of one core on a visible view and 0.10 % with the overlay hidden (3 and 2 windows of 10 s). Soak of 603 s cycling the 13 tabs with a note of 3 s on each: 106 samples, every one the right view on `renderer: gpu` with slot 0, the MPC pid and the helper pid unchanged, MPC VmRSS 946556 to 947120 kB, helper 16160 kB, no new `dmesg` line, `gpu_helper_restarts: 0`, the re-report counter did not move (14, all from the debug tests). Play arrival over a full screen page (XL, 2026-11-21, `.hw-local/mpc-perf/xl-pageguard-2026-11-21/`, also with the guard off): the helper hid its plane 71 to 106 ms after the picture (blank instance frame, `instance of the page not identified`) and brought it back 142 to 336 ms later (mostly 230 ms), once per arrival; the grace time of the blank picture is 600 ms, the test host redraws in 200 ms.
  - All 13 views on their tabs with `renderer: gpu`, helper running; kernel state of plane 38 each time: `crtc=crtc-0`, `fb` allocated by `synthyboy-overl` (the helper's process name), `format=AR24`, `imported=yes`, `modifier=0x0`, `crtc-pos` equal to the region (232x1168+124+56, 240x600+140+340, 240x512+140+384, 222x1230+178+27); on the Macros and Sub/Nois tabs `hidden: no view on screen`, plane off. Start-up of the helper (spawn to `ready`): 62 to 85 ms over eight starts; the helper has one thread, `Cpus_allowed_list` 0-1, `VmRSS` about 16 MB, ordinary scheduling.
  - The pictures, seen through the helper's own read-back (`/tmp/synthyboy-overlay-gpu-dump`, converted with `raw2png.py`, looked at one view type after the other): the filter curve with the resonance peak, grid, 0 dB line and the translucent fill (Filter 2 off: the flat line at 0 dB), the LFO sine with the cursor line and the two discs, the sawtooth cycle of Oscillator 1 (Oscillators 2 and 3 are off in this preset: the empty frame), the envelope dot with its vertical line and the two painted-over marker squares on a transparent plane. Compared with the CPU renderer for the same 256 dB values of Filter 1 (dump of the plugin and read-back of the helper taken together, nothing moving): the centre of the line differs by 0.02 pixels on average over 950 columns, at most 0.42 pixels (at the peak), 0.4 percent of the pixels differ by more than 24 of 255 (edges of the grid lines and of the peak). The orientation on the glass and the smoothness were not seen by an eye (the screenshots show the primary plane only).
  - CPU in percent of one core over windows of six seconds with a note held (Filter 1: LFO to cutoff; LFO 1 cursor; Oscillator 1 frame modulated by LFO 1, amount 45; Envelope 2 with an attack of 10 seconds), the overlay thread of the plugin plus the helper process, against the CPU drawing of the same build with `/tmp/synthyboy-overlay-nogpu` in the same state: Filter 1 curve 22.77 (CPU) against 6.27 + 2.15 = 8.42 (GPU, 27.6 pictures a second presented); LFO cursor 4.96 against 0.33 + 2.31 = 2.64 (29.7 a second); modulated oscillator 7.77 against 0.83 + 2.31 = 3.14 (29.8 a second); envelope dot 7.62 against 0.66 + 1.65 = 2.31 (26.1 a second). At rest 0.17 to 0.33 for the thread and 0.00 for the helper (it sleeps), the same as with the CPU. The share of the thread with the GPU is nearly all the full computations of the curve (limited to 8 percent of a core), which the GPU does not take over. With 16.7 milliseconds between scenes (`SYNTHYBOY_MPC_OVERLAY_GPU_FRAME_MS=16.7`, plugin md5 `a4170a2bd88164695765824ad448dd95`): Filter 1 6.94 + 4.13 = 11.07 with 54.5 pictures a second, LFO cursor 0.50 + 3.64 = 4.14 with 50.4 a second; the default stays 33.3. The GPU frequency (`devfreq`, `cur_freq`) was 100 MHz, the lowest step, in every one-second sample of all runs; the temperature of the two zones 65 to 69 degrees. The helper's counters: render (draw, `glFinish`, swap, import) 2.8 to 3.0 ms for the Filter 1 picture in steady state (4.1 ms for the first picture), SETPLANE 0.5 to 16 ms (it waits for the vblank), skipped vblanks 0 in every run; SETPLANE calls longer than 20 ms (two vblanks, the host's own flip in front of ours): 0 at rest, 19 of 180 (LFO cursor) with the note held at 30 a second, 52 of 305 at 60 a second.
  - Faults, a note held, Filter 1 moving: `kill -9` of the helper: the status file said `renderer: cpu` and the plane was the CPU's (framebuffer allocated by `sb-overlay`) 0.18 s after the kill, the helper was started again 1.15 s after, `renderer: gpu` at 1.31 s, `gpu_failures 1`, `gpu_last_error` "the channel to the helper closed (the helper died)", the picture counters went on (frames_total 1167 -> 1198 in the following second), the MPC pid stayed. `touch /tmp/synthyboy-overlay-nogpu`: CPU at 0.33 s, no helper process left; `rm`: GPU at 0.66 s (no failure counted for a planned stop). `kill -STOP` of the helper (a hang): CPU within 0.94 s (0.7 s without an acknowledgement, then killed and cleaned), restarted after the back-off. No new `dmesg` line in any of these. `systemctl stop acvs` with the helper running: 0.39 s, no MPC and no helper process left, plane 38 `crtc=(null)`; `kill -9` of the MPC app: the helper left by itself 0.34 s later (its socket closed), plane 38 off.
  - Audio, statistics build with the helper, a note of 75 seconds held while the helper was started (Macros to Filter 1), the views animated, the helper killed, hung, switched off and on, the views changed: 33312 callbacks, every gap in the bucket below 1.5 times the block time (none at 1.5 times or more), largest gap 3269 us (1.13 times), callback average 312 and longest 1267 us.
  - Soak of 1206 seconds cycling all 13 views with a note of 3 seconds on each (216 samples): the MPC pid stayed 11389, the helper pid stayed 11572 (no restart, `gpu_failures` 0), `VmRSS` of the MPC process between 944916 and 946044 kB and of the helper between 16128 and 16164 kB, every sample showed the view of its tab with `renderer: gpu`, the helper presented 9479 pictures, no new `dmesg` line (1713 lines before and after, no panfrost or drm line), temperature of the two zones 66.9 to 69.6 and 65.0 to 67.3 degrees, `/data` free 2.4 GB. Full screen menu (double tap on the value of Filter 1 Model): plane 38 off, back after closing it.

- Spectrum curve over the meters of the Macros tab (section "Spectrum", "The curve over the meters"; the view `spectrum` of the overlay), measured on the Key 61 on 2026-11-14 (arm32 plugin md5 `eb7172e2541289abdc849abd878ca169`, helper `ce097f58634368cb1097b0d368fc4426`, project `Sbtest`, preset `Acid Saw Reso`, 44.1 kHz, one instance; the audio test with the same code plus the callback statistics, plugin md5 `bb7dc0db3bac276a7018eb1560810d64`; raw results and the helper's frames are kept next to the measurement notes of the project):
  - On the Macros tab the status file says `state: visible`, `view: spectrum`, `renderer: gpu`, `instance_slot: 0`, `markers_read_spectrum` equal to the expected `6b3a2c` and `3a6b64`, `instance_pixel_read: 1414d8`; the kernel state of plane 38 is `crtc-pos` 136x1208+590+36 (the region 1208 x 136 at x 36, y 590 of the landscape UI); on Sub/Nois the plane is off (`hidden: no view on screen`). All 13 other views were visited once more with the new skin and plugin: each on its tab with `renderer: gpu`, slot 0 and its own plane rectangle.
  - Where a single sustained note puts the peak (the point of the 256 with the highest level, in Hz, against the pitch of the note; a point is 2.7 percent wide): note 36 at 64.1 Hz for 65.4 Hz (0.74 points away), note 72 at 530.3 Hz for 523.3 Hz (0.50), note 84 at 1043.9 Hz for 1046.5 Hz (0.09), note 96 at 2111.3 Hz for 2093.0 Hz (0.32). For notes 48 and 60 the highest point is a harmonic, not the fundamental (the preset has Filter 1 at 450 Hz and a resonance of 78): 393.7 Hz is 3.01 times 130.8 Hz and 516.2 Hz is 1.97 times 261.6 Hz.
  - The helper's own read-back of its finished frame, converted to a picture and looked at: the line at the floor with the three faint grid lines at rest; a sawtooth on note 36 with its first partials as rounded humps; a single peak with two smaller ones on note 84; a chord of five notes with the partials running up to about 3 kHz. The converted read-back looked as intended (low frequencies on the left, the gradient fill, the 2.5 pixel line). Compared with the CPU renderer for the same 256 levels (the plugin's dump of the levels and the helper's frame taken in the same second): for a steady note C2 0.85 percent of the pixels differ by more than 24 of 255 and the centre of the line differs by 0.067 pixels on average, 0.50 at most; for C6 0.34 percent, 0.043 and 4.0 (one column at the sharp peak); for the moving chord 3.9 percent and 2.3 pixels, because the two dumps are up to a second apart while the curve moves. How the picture looks to the eye on the glass was not seen (the screenshots show the primary plane only).
  - The 32 meters underneath: a screenshot of the primary plane during the chord shows the 32 meters with bars of different heights on about the columns 7 to 24, the three marker squares are the only visible trace of the overlay's skin there.
  - After a note of 3 seconds (samples once a second from the start of the note): `spectrum_max_level` 0.66 while it sounds, 0.43 at 3.7 seconds, 0.024 at 4.9 seconds and exactly 0 at 6.1 seconds; the pictures presented per second go 5 (the note starting), 30, 30, 30, 20, 0 and the picture counter then stands still at 2392 for the following 8 seconds, `spectrum_ffts_total` stopped at 1914 from 3.7 seconds on (no FFT is run for a silent window). One FFT of 4096 points with the mapping to the 256 points takes 0.54 milliseconds on average on this CPU (`spectrum_analysis_ms`).
  - CPU in percent of one core, windows of 10 seconds, the overlay thread plus the helper process: at rest on the Macros tab 0.40 + 0.00 (the helper sleeps); a held chord (5 notes) 1.89 + 2.18 = 4.07 with 29.7 pictures a second, no skipped vblank, 17 SETPLANE calls of 299 longer than 20 ms; a held high note 1.89 + 2.18 = 4.07, 29.6 a second. The whole MPC process was at 28 percent at rest and 60 percent with the chord (the synthesis). The CPU renderer (the fallback, `/tmp/synthyboy-overlay-nogpu`) with the chord: 15.9 percent for the thread (render 4.4 milliseconds, rotate 1.4, SETPLANE 8.4 per picture, 29.6 pictures a second), 0.37 at rest.
  - A helper of another protocol version (the helper of the previous package, protocol 1, put next to this plugin by file move and restarted through the switch): the status says `renderer: cpu`, `gpu_last_error` "the helper reports: protocol version mismatch", four failures and `exhausted` after about 4 seconds; the CPU picture stayed on the plane the whole time (31 pictures a second with a note held, the plane's kernel state unchanged), the MPC process id did not change; with the right helper back and the switch off and on it was `renderer: gpu` and `running` again.
  - Audio, the statistics build with the helper, a chord of five notes held for 80 seconds with the curve moving, three round trips Macros to Filter 1 and back during it (the helper shows and hides the plane each time): 41718 callbacks since the plugin was loaded (about 125 seconds), every gap in the bucket below 1.5 times the block time (none at 1.5 times or more), largest gap 3377 microseconds (1.16 times), callback average 500 and longest 1291 microseconds.
  - Soak of 607 seconds: 45 samples on the Macros tab with a note of 1.5 to 3 seconds every 4 seconds (single notes and chords, random), and four passes and a part of a fifth through all 13 other tabs with a note of 3 seconds on each (62 samples) and a visit of Sub/Nois after each pass: every sample showed the view of its tab with `renderer: gpu`, the MPC process id stayed 2744 and the helper's 2869 (no restart, `gpu_helper_restarts` 0, `gpu_last_error` none), `VmRSS` of the MPC process between 944508 and 945072 kB and of the helper between 16236 and 16256 kB, `dmesg` 1698 lines before and after (no new line), the temperature of the two zones 65.0 to 69.2 degrees, `/data` free 2496 MB all the time.

- Overlay on the MPC XL and on the Key 61 with run-time discovery (section "Overlay (experimental)", "Discovery" and "Pacing"), measured on 2026-11-14. Package `ov9-both`: arm64 plugin md5 `6724e4e4d29cc8a5ebb8de899ebd58bc` with helper `70a26a102fca428af5ed6fd8734027a0` on an MPC XL (aarch64, Linux 6.18 PREEMPT_RT, 44.1 kHz, project `Sbtest` of that device with the preset `Init`, one instance, no modulation route in it), arm32 plugin md5 `aa0f74cb183f41036eb7cc1add92ae41` with helper `76ede1560cd06b444e06ebf26c581d8b` on the Key 61 (project `Sbtest`, preset `Acid Saw Reso`). The same package folder serves both devices. The XL measurements below (the CPU numbers, the faults, the take-over of the plane, the pressure, the audio statistics, the first minute of pace) were taken with the build of the same sources before the last change, the planning of the cores (arm64 plugin md5 `94c922e77e2472aa8620f8d8f4536760`, the helper is the same file; `planCpus`: the isolated cores are always kept off, the cores of the audio threads only while others are left, a pure function with its own tests); the 14 views, a minute of pace and a soak of 907 s were repeated with the final package, and the Key 61 was measured with the final one.
  - What the status file says it found, XL: `/dev/dri/card0`, CRTC 82 (index 0, found by the primary plane 34), the application's planes 34 and 40, free planes 46 52 58 64 70 76, plane 46 used, panel 800x1280, the mode says 60 Hz and 56.463 Hz are measured (period 17.7108 ms over 40 vblanks, the whole discovery 717.6 ms, once), stride 2, rotation 270 found from the markers (`rotation_source: found from the markers`), cores 0-3 allowed and 4-7 avoided (the audio threads AudioWorker0 to AudioWorker3 and Audio Processing are pinned there; nothing is isolated), the GL renderer `Mali-G610 (Panfrost)` on `/dev/dri/renderD128`, helper start-up 70 ms. Key 61: CRTC 37, primary plane 33, application planes 33 and 35, free planes 38 and 40, plane 38 used, 60.062 Hz measured (period 16.6493 ms, discovery 672.7 ms), stride 2, rotation 90 found, cores 0-1 allowed (2-3 isolated by the kernel; the audio threads sit on all four cores, so only the isolated ones are kept off), `Mali-T760 (Panfrost)`, helper start-up 122 ms. Rotation 270 on the XL is found by the markers alone: the survey of the same day had it from known pixels of the UI.
  - XL, the 13 view tabs and the Macros tab (all 14 views): on every tab `state: visible`, the tab's view, `renderer: gpu`, `instance_slot: 0`, `instance_pixel_read: 1414d8`, and the kernel state of the plane (`/sys/kernel/debug/dri/0/state`) `crtc=video_port3`, `format=AR24` and `crtc-pos` where the region of the view is under rotation 270: Filter 1 and 2 232x1168+444+56, LFO 1 to 4 240x600+420+340, Osc 1 to 3 240x512+420+384, Env 1 to 4 222x1230+400+23, Spectrum 136x1208+74+36 (the Key 61 has the same sizes at its own positions: 232x1168+124+56 and so on); on the Sub/Nois tab the state is `hidden: no view on screen` and the plane is off; the Key 61 gave all 14 again with `renderer: gpu` and the positions of the earlier proof. The helper's read-back frames were converted (rotation 270 for the XL) and looked at, one of each type: the low pass curve with its grid lines upright and the fill under it (knee at the right edge for 12 kHz), the sine of LFO 1 with the cursor line and dot on the curve, the sawtooth cycle of Osc 1, the envelope dot at the top left (the picture is transparent but for the dot and the line), the spectrum curve of a three note chord. What cannot be judged from them (how it looks on the glass) is in the list at the end of the report of that day.
  - Pace, XL, `pacetest` (`.hw-local/mpc-tools/overlay-probe/xl/pacetest.c`, a transparent buffer on a free plane through the borrowed descriptor, nothing visible; intervals counted in vblanks at the return of the call; logs in `.hw-local/mpc-perf/xl-ov9/pacetest_series.txt` and `busy_test.txt`). With the application's UI moving (first the night's main page with the live meters, later a knob dragged on the screen): updates back to back 548 in 15 s, 36.5 a second, 248 intervals of one vblank and 299 of two (17.6 and 35.4 ms); a call 0.5 ms after a vblank: 272 intervals of two vblanks and 6 of three; 3 ms after: 220 and 41; 9 ms after: 113 and 112; with the knob dragged: back to back 46.9 a second (449 of one, 113 of two, 1 of three), 3 ms after a vblank 302 of two and 24 of three; a call the moment the vblank wait returns, one on every second vblank (`gridfree 2`): 422 of 422 intervals exactly two vblanks (28.20 a second, the call takes 17.72 ms: one vblank) and 338 of 338 with the knob dragged; on every third: 281 of 281 exactly three. With a static page on the screen the same back to back updates are 846 of 846 exactly one vblank (56.46 a second) and every delay of 0.2 to 9 ms, the same framebuffer, a one pixel move, a second thread and the blocking atomic update give 422 of 422 (338 of 338) too: so what alternates is the competition with the application's own commits. The application's descriptor does have the atomic capability (an empty test-only request: return 0); a non-blocking atomic update of the plane only returns in 0.03 ms (median), one per vblank returns clean with a static page (677 of 677) and still alternates with the UI moving; when it reaches the screen was not measured (no completion event without the application's queue).
  - Pace in the plugin, XL, a minute of LFO 1 with a note held (`pace_*` of the status file, the intervals between two plane updates of the helper): 1697 intervals, 1693 of them two vblanks (the 34 to 36 ms bucket) and 4 of three (one late plane update), 28.1 pictures a second (an earlier minute of the same code with the helper alone changed: 1698 of 1698). Before the plugin handed over its scenes first in every step and kept its steps on a fixed rhythm: 1568 of two, 4 of three and 64 of four vblanks (a scene was late once a second: the page detection and the plane check wait for the lock the plane update holds). Key 61 the same way, a minute: 1814 of 1823 two vblanks, 8 of three, 1 of four, 30.2 pictures a second. The CPU drawing on the XL (the file switch, 27 pictures in the last second): 60 of 60 intervals two vblanks.
  - CPU in percent of one core, windows of 10.8 s on the XL (overlay thread + helper), at rest on any view tab 0.4 + 0.00; moving: Filter 1 with the pad pressure swept 6.18 + 1.84 (18.2 pictures a second: the full computations of the curve are limited to a share of a core), LFO 1 cursor 0.46 + 2.49 (26.5 a second), Osc 1 with the Frame knob dragged 0.46 + 0.18 (2.1 pictures a second: the table barely changes), Env 1 with 0.35 s notes back to back 0.65 + 1.66 (19.4 a second), Spectrum with a three note chord 3.32 + 2.40 (26.6 a second). Key 61, windows of 6 s, same code: rest 0.17 to 0.50 + 0.00, Filter 1 moving 6.60 + 2.64 (30.0 a second), LFO 1 cursor 0.50 + 2.48 (30.2); the earlier numbers of the day before were 6.27 + 2.15 and 0.33 + 2.31: the differences are within 0.5 of a point of one core. The GPU stayed at its lowest frequency on both. Cores, sampled every 0.1 s in all of these windows (the core each thread last ran on, `/proc/<pid>/task/<tid>/stat`): the overlay thread and the helper only on cores 0 to 3 on the XL (none on 4 to 7).
  - Audio, XL, statistics build (arm64 plugin md5 `0055cceba44c3795689d212433959b28`, the same helper; `nominal_block_us` 2902) with a five note chord held for 100 seconds and a sixth note whose polyphonic pressure swept the filter (6034 pressure events), during it: the views Filter 1, LFO 1, Osc 1, Env 1, Filter 2 and the Macros tab one after the other, the helper killed with SIGKILL, the file switch on and off, the helper stopped with SIGSTOP (killed by the plugin after the time-out and restarted): 49 796 callbacks, every one of the 49 795 gaps in the bucket below 1.5 times the block time, the largest 3323 us (1.14 times), callback time average 80 us, largest 215 us.
  - Faults, XL (the helper killed with SIGKILL, switched off with the file, stopped with SIGSTOP, with a note held and the picture moving): after SIGKILL the CPU picture is on the plane within 0.2 s and the helper is back at 1.25 s (back-off of one second); the switch gives the CPU picture within 1.1 s and the helper again within 1.1 s of removing the file (a planned stop counts no failure); the stopped helper is found after 1.5 s (no acknowledgement in 0.7 s), the CPU draws and the helper is back 2 s later; MPC pid unchanged and no new `dmesg` line in any of them. Another program takes the plane (`pacetest take` puts a framebuffer of its own on plane 46 while the plugin shows a static picture): the helper finds it within a second (`SETPLANE: plane 46 reports crtc ...`), the plugin switches to plane 52 (`display_plane_switches: 1`, the status names it), the CPU picture is on plane 52 1.4 s after the take and the helper's again 2.5 s after it; the plane that was taken is not touched by the plugin.
  - Pad pressure, XL: with a held note and a pressure of 127 the live cutoff of Filter 1 in the status goes from 12000 Hz to 20000 Hz and the curve hash changes (the helper's frames: the knee of the low pass moves to the right edge); with the sweep the live cutoff went through 13434, 17140 and 20000 Hz.
  - Soak, XL, 907 seconds on the final package: 165 samples (the Macros tab with a 1.5 to 3 s note every 4 s, and the 13 other tabs with a 3 s note each, several rounds, Sub/Nois after each), every one the tab's view on `renderer: gpu` on plane 46; MPC pid 44658 and helper pid 44881 unchanged, no restart, VmRSS of the MPC 402 604 kB and of the helper 12 880 kB at every sample, `dmesg` 1734 lines before and after, 67.5 to 68.4 degrees C. Key 61, 312 seconds: 54 samples, every one the right view on `renderer: gpu`, MPC pid 9999 and helper pid 10128 unchanged, no restart, VmRSS 945 476 to 946 040 kB and 16 272 kB, `dmesg` 1685 lines before and after.
- **The Play page, Key 61 (3.10.1.12, arm32) and MPC XL (arm64), same code (2026-11-14).** The skin with 39 tabs in 11 function keys loads on both (36 and 10 before), the Play key is the first, the status file says `view: play1` / `play2` / `play3` with `renderer: gpu`. The helper's finished frames show the three panels with their titles and the frame of the sub-tab. A selector drag changes the title and the data hash of its panel only (the same hashes on both devices); the eight knobs jump to the selected instance (Env 3 attack 2465.4 against Env 1 attack 5.0, set through the proxy knob and read on the Env 3 tab, and the other way round: a change on the Env 3 tab shows on the Play knob). With a held note the envelope dot moves along the long attack of Env 3 and the LFO cursor runs. The overlay thread uses about 0.4 percent of a core at rest and 0.6 to 0.9 percent with a note, the helper below one percent; a soak of 10 minutes with 382 (Key 61) and 432 (XL) sub-tab and selector changes: no state change, no GPU failure, no helper restart, the application's process id unchanged. Key 61 with the callback statistics build and three held notes for 66 seconds with 47 changes: 22719 callbacks, no gap of 1.5 blocks or more (the largest was 1.22 blocks). Not done on a device: an LFO to wavetable position route.
- **Project load with the Play page's selectors, Key 61 (3.10.1.12, arm32, 2026-11-14), the debug log of `shell-mpc/play_log.h`.** A project that holds a chunk with `mpcPlayOsc` 2, `mpcPlayEnv` 3, `mpcPlayLfo` 4 (the chunk the plugin itself returned from an edited instance: Env 3 attack 2465.4, release 6143, Osc 2 level 100, WT Position 55.5; the project file was made from `Sbtest.xpj` by replacing the plugin's state blob, because a project cannot be saved by touch: the hardware Save button has no touch route, see below) was opened from the project list. The host's calls, in order: `effSetProgram(2)`, 73 ms later `effSetChunk` (50191 bytes), then 1026 ms after the start two `getParameter` calls and `setParameter(0, 1.0)`, one more `getParameter` and `setParameter(0, <the value it had read>)`; nothing else (no write-back of the other parameters, no write to a selector or a proxy, no second program selection). The state dump after the load (every engine parameter with nine digits, the three selectors, the proxies and the whole chunk the plugin returns) is identical, line for line, to the dump of the instance the project was made from: Osc 2 / Env 3 / LFO 4 and every edited value are back. The host's toggle of parameter 0 would have turned a master volume of -12 into -11.9999981 in the chunk (the normalised round trip is not exact); `writeRealParam` takes the value the parameter had before the toggle exactly ("Project save and reload"). Not observed: the host's calls when it SAVES (`effGetChunk`, `getParameter`), a project that the host itself saved with non-default selectors, a second reload after changing the selectors, and host-side automation recording of a proxy (the transport and the Save button are hardware buttons; a virtual keyboard through `/dev/uinput` was not allowed). The pull-down panel (swipe down from the top edge) has the `Automation` switch (Read / Write) on the touch screen, but no transport.
- **Function key labels, Key 61 (2026-11-14).** With the labels of the overlay skin ("Function keys and sub-tabs") the first row of keys reads PLAY, MACROS, OSC, MIX, FILTER with the sub-tab dots under each; after a second press of the Osc key the key reads OSC1 SHP: the key shows the `tabName` of the current sub-tab.
- **Destroying and re-creating the plugin instance, Key 61 (3.10.1.12, arm32, 2026-11-14).** Deleting a track is only possible from Main mode, which cannot be reached by touch on this device; the substitute that goes through the same `effClose` is replacing the track's instrument in Sounds mode (left-edge handle panel with "Show Mode Shortcut Panel", switched on for the test and off again, the preference page left with its back arrow) and putting SynthyBoy back. 30 swaps (a stock instrument: Bassline, Mini D, Odyssey, Hype, Solina, Organ, in turn; SynthyBoy again; the overlay view on screen before the swap: Play, Macros (spectrum), Osc 1, Filter 1, Env 1; a note held through the swap): after every destroy the status file says `instances: 0` (0.1 to 0.7 s after the tap), the GPU helper is gone (no process), the plane is off, the MPC pid is the same; after every re-creation exactly one instance and one helper, `visible`, `renderer: gpu`; 0 failures, 0 new `dmesg` lines, no core file, no restart of the unit in the journal. 17 project switches through the Browser (handle panel, folder `SbtestSwitch` with copies of `Sbtest.xpj` and `Sbtest2.xpj`, Load, "Don't save"; the original `Sbtest.xpj` untouched, its modification time is of 2026-11-13) on Play, Macros, Osc 1, Filter 1 and Env 1: every load made one new instance (journal), the helper count never above 1 and 0 while no instance existed, same pid, `visible` and `renderer: gpu` afterwards (in 16 of 17 the polling saw `instances: 0` in between, once the gap was shorter than the polling step). 5 stops of the app with the overlay visible (`systemctl stop acvs`, 0.6 s each, the unit "Deactivated successfully"): no MPC and no helper process afterwards, plane off, 0 `dmesg` lines, no core file; the app started again with the overlay visible (`gpu`). A 5 minute soak on the final package: 300 s, 216 steps, 0 deviations, the same MPC pid, `gpu_failures 0`, `gpu_helper_restarts 0`.
- **Bug found by that test (fixed): the overlay stayed dark after a re-creation that came after a long run.** The status file said `instances: 0` and the kill switch had no effect for as long as the earlier run of the overlay thread had lasted (the thread's clock started at zero again, the controller's deadlines did not); see "The overlay thread after the last instance closed".


Browser artwork tile in Sounds > INSTRUMENTS, tried on the MPC XL on 2026-11-14 (MPC 3.10.1.12, trial image `tile-mock/tile-mock-270x110.png` copied to `Plugin Skins/browser_images/soundsmode.png`; the trial file did not stay on the machine):

- The 270 x 110 PNG appears cleanly in the Sounds > INSTRUMENTS browser on the second page of instruments in place of the text tile, at the same size as the stock instrument tiles.
- The 540 x 220 PNG (2x supersampled) is cropped to the top-left 270 x 110 pixels rather than scaled down by the host; larger resolutions offer no benefit.
- The 270 x 110 PNG is drawn with 1:1 pixel accuracy on the XL screen without scaling blur; fine lines and small labels remain sharp and legible.
- The host required restarting the application before the newly copied artwork tile was shown.
- Tapping the tile to open the preset list (`.xpl`) was not measured (the package does not include `.xpl` presets; presets are exposed as VST2 programs). Appearance on the MPC Key 61 was not measured during this session.

- **Oscillator UI on the Key 61 (round 14, `.hw-local/mpc-perf/overlay-pkgs/ov13-osc-ui-results/`, package `ov13-osc-ui`, 2026-11-15).** The touch screen is the node `/dev/input/event0` (ILI2117, multitouch protocol B, ABS 0 .. 2048 on both axes, `PROP=0`: no `INPUT_PROP_DIRECT`, found by `BTN_TOUCH`); the overlay's panel 800 x 1280 and rotation 90 came from its own discovery. Taps and drags injected with `mtouch` came out of the decoder at the injected pixel (a real capture of the node is the fixture of `touch_host`). By touch: all five instances of the compact selector, the type pictograms (Wavetable, Multisample, Granular), the full-screen button, CLOSE, every detail page (unison of the three types, grain window, multisample envelope), the route popup (target stepper over the seven destinations, balance enabled for Filter blend, BUS knobs, close by the button and by the backdrop), the modulations page (long press, add, depth, polarity, bypass); every control moved the parameter the mapping names (read back with the plugin's own parameter dump). That the host's own widgets and an automation recording follow a write of the overlay pages (`audioMasterAutomate` is called by the display thread) was NOT looked at on the device. The Q targets of every group were read from the status file; Q 1 .. Q 4 written through the plugin's setParameter (debug file `/tmp/synthyboy-q-fake-write`, the host's own mapping of a Q-Link to the proxy is NOT exercised: a knob cannot be turned from outside) reached the four parameters of the Unison group (frame 0 -> 50, voices 1 -> 7, detune 25 -> 60, blend 80 -> 25 for 0.5 / 0.4 / 0.6 / 0.25). Sound: with LEVEL dragged to 0 the plugin's own spectrum (Macros tab, two held notes) read peak 0.000, at 72.5 it read 0.926.
- **Host widgets under a full screen.** With the full screen open the host picture has no Env / LFO selector and no knob of the Env sub-tab (screenshots `15`, `17`); drags across both selector sliders changed nothing (Play Env 5 and Play LFO 1 before and after), the same drags without the full screen changed them (Env 5 -> 2, LFO 1 -> 9); eight drags on the eight knob places of the Env sub-tab changed none of the eight Env parameters with the full screen open (all eight changed without it). Leaving the page with the full screen open (Macros key) closed the stack (`ui_auto_closes` 1) and the widgets were back on return (the first version of this round left them hidden: the instance went on reporting the hidden frame after the stack was closed behind its back; fixed, `closeUiWhenLeft` tells the instance).
- **Renderer.** The GPU helper (Mali-T760, Panfrost) drew the new Line, Arc and Frame ops; against the CPU renderer's picture of the same scene (the parameter dump of the device rendered by `PlayView::render` on the Mac, `frames/gpu-vs-cpu.json`) 0.01 to 0.4 percent of the pixels differ by more than 16 of 255 on the pages without a dense wavetable polyline (anti-aliasing of the arcs and lines, maximum 38 on a page of knobs), 1.2 to 1.5 percent where the wavetable polyline of a warped cycle is in the picture. The previous difference on steep edges (where clamped miter narrowed the GPU strip to zero width while the CPU stroked per column) has been fixed in `shell-mpc/overlay/gpu/stroke_strip.h`: for turns sharper than miter limit 2.5 (`cosHalf < 0.4`), an SVG-style bevel join (four vertices: P ± n1*ext then P ± n2*ext) is emitted, preserving full arm width. The CPU fallback of the whole canvas (switch `/tmp/synthyboy-overlay-nogpu`) works: 16 pictures a second, the overlay thread 37 to 41 percent of one core.
- **CPU, GPU renderer** (one-core percent over 8 s windows, `meas.sh`): overlay thread / helper: compact view idle 1.4 / 0.0; compact, three held notes 2.0 / 4.7; full screen idle 1.6 / 0.0; full screen, three held notes 2.2 / 5.3; full screen, notes held and knobs dragged without a pause 2.3 / 5.3; 30 pictures a second while something moves. The helper draws only when the picture changes.
- **Soak** (package with the callback statistics, `ov13-osc-ui-stats`, otherwise the same; four notes held all the time, a touch action every five seconds: knobs, route popup, unison page, type changes, modulations page, instance changes): 316 s, 64 action rounds, 108542 audio callbacks, every gap below 1.5 times the nominal block time (largest 3557 us against 2902 us, 1.23 times), no gap of 1.5 times or more, process time 447 us on average and 1325 us at most, dmesg unchanged, no helper restart or failure.

- **Oscillator UI, round 15 (`.hw-local/mpc-perf/overlay-pkgs/ov14-osc-ui-results/`, package `ov14-osc-ui`).** The full screen, the detail pages and the popups are opaque over the whole region now (composed picture `composed-51-full-A.png`: no host graphic, no Q-Link frame); the compact view covers the third. The list popup opens from the source dropdown and from the names of the warp type steppers by touch, a tap on the 5th row wrote `osc1.wavetable` 4, the backdrop closed it. The Tune group's Q 1 and Q 2 written through the plugin's setParameter (debug file): Q 1 = 0.625 gave transpose 12, then Q 2 = 14/22 gave 15, then Q 1 = 0.25 gave -21 (octave -2, remainder 3 kept). The host's own widgets follow a write of the overlay path: with the OSC tab on screen, a write through `uiWrite` (debug file `/tmp/synthyboy-ui-fake-write`) made the host's LEVEL knob show 21.5 and TRANSPOSE 7 within a second (`screens/61`); a drag of the full screen's LEVEL showed 49.4 on the OSC tab opened afterwards. Not reached: an automation RECORDING (it needs the hardware record and play keys, nothing can press them from outside). CPU renderer: the long vertical lines at steep edges are gone (the stroke's reach was stretched by the slope; now capped); against the GPU frame 1.0 percent (main) and 2.8 percent (full screen, which also includes the other Q frame and a SEM field of a different state) of the canvas differ by more than 16 of 255 with a seven-tooth Sync polyline in the picture, inside the picture rectangle alone 4 to 6 percent (the edges of near vertical teeth are anti-aliased differently; not the level of the scenes without such a polyline, 0.01 to 0.4 percent).
- **Overlay drawing: the frame budget (D plan, trial 17, Key 61, `.hw-local/mpc-perf/overlay-pkgs/ov16-gpu-bench-results/`, package `ov16-gpu-bench`).** The helper of the measuring build (`SYNTHYBOY_MPC_OVERLAY_BENCH`) draws a scene of its own from `/tmp/sb-bench.conf` over the whole Play region (1280 x 800 UI pixels) while four notes are held; 800 pixel wide curves, windows of 6 seconds, the GPU clock is `simple_ondemand` (100 to 600 MHz, so the lightly loaded steps run at the lowest clock and the steps near the limit are noisy). The pictures go out on the pace grid of 30 a second; a picture that takes more than about 17 ms loses every second slot (15 a second).

Read from the table: a curve costs about half a millisecond and a little more than half a millisecond per thousand vertices, whatever its width (0.5 to 0.75 pixels cost the same as 1 pixel); a full region translucent layer costs about 13 ms at the idle clock and 2.8 ms at full clock; the soft extra pass of a glow costs about 0.9 ms per curve while the bloom (a quarter size layer, blurred, added) costs a flat 4 to 7 ms, cheaper from about six curves; 4 x MSAA is available on the plane (an EGL configuration with 4 samples works, +1.9 ms on an empty picture); the feedback layer (afterglow) costs nothing measurable but accumulates the bloom. The hang callback's longest gap stays at 1.18 to 1.22 times the nominal block in every step (ov13: 1.23). The 2 minute peak run and the temperature series were not made (the budget was enough). The pace of 60 pictures a second was not reached: with `stride=1` an empty picture still comes at 30 a second on the Play page (the cause was not found).

**The budget (Key 61, 30 pictures a second, no worse audio): a picture that costs at most about 14 ms in the helper.** That holds: about 8 curves of 800 points or 16 curves of 200 to 400 points, any thickness, plus 40 to 100 texts, about 30 rounded rectangles, one bloom inside a display rectangle, at most one full region translucent layer. The protocol's frame (65 536 vertices, 128 curves) is accepted and does not harm the audio, but costs 62 ms a picture here (11 a second).

- **The finished UI on the Key 61 (D plan, trial 18, package `ov16-osc-ui`, results `ov16-osc-ui-results/`).** Package built from the export of the (historic) commit with the limiter ceiling lifted and +18 dB; the app ran without a preload; the Key 61 chose the level of detail "reduced" by itself (the pictures of the helper take longer than fourteen milliseconds while the picture moves), "full" and "reduced" by file for the table below. One core percent, 8 s windows, three held notes, the full screen of instance A; "picture ms" is the last picture of the helper at the end of the window (the GPU clock moves between 100 and 600 MHz, an idle picture is drawn at the lowest clock and is slow):

(The table of this trial, with negative CPU percentages and empty rows, came from a measuring script that read counters across a restart of the helper: replaced by the table of trial 19.)

5 minute soak with the statistics package (`ov16-osc-ui-stats`: four held notes all the time, every 5 seconds a touch action: instance, full screen open / close, knob drag): 311 s, 110 700 audio callbacks, the longest gap 4 317 us against the nominal 2 902 us (1.49 x, every gap in the bucket below 1.5 x; ov13 had 1.23 x), processing time mean 272 us and at most 2 122 us, the MPC and helper processes the same ones all the way (no restart), 54 of 54 samples `visible` / `gpu`.
